GAME PUSH NOTIFICATION CONTENT — 365 DAYS × 3 DAILY SLOTS = 1,095 NOTIFICATIONS
Language: English | Local device time | Schedule: 08:00, 12:00, 20:00
CTA for all notifications: Play Now
GAME_ICON: {{RANDOM_GAME_ICON}} (choose at send time from available game icons)

Notes: DAY is a rotation index (1–365), not a mandatory calendar date. Select a game and its icon together. Respect OS notification permissions and local-time scheduling.
